Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the time structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 521. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 845 and converges at rate O(1/n^3).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 521 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the time structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 845. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
